- **mri-gradient-hardware-constraint-setup**: Use when designing time-optimal MRI gradient waveforms (e.g., diffusion DWI, phase-contrast flow encoding) and you must specify hardware bounds gmax/smax, raster time dt, waveform size N, and moment/eddy-current constraints as GrOpt solver inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mri-gradient-hardware-constraint-setup))
- **mri-gradient-moment-nulling**: Use when designing MRI gradient waveforms that must be fully or partially moment-nulled (e.g., b=1000 mm2/s six-direction diffusion encoding on GE MR750/EPIC or Siemens Skyra/IDEA) within specific Gmax/Smax hardware limits and a time-optimal duration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mri-gradient-moment-nulling))
- **mri-gradient-waveform-optimization**: Use when a pulse sequence needs a gradient waveform generated on-the-fly (within ~1-40 ms) subject to hardware limits (Gmax, Smax), gradient moment nulling (M0/M1/M2), eddy-current nulling, or PNS constraints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mri-gradient-waveform-optimization))

Prevents silent errors in units, frames, field components, harmonic normalization, bounded optimization and limit definitions. Ships tested SI helpers checked against analytic cases. Not clinical advice. ([Joker-ZXR/my-skills](https://github.com/Joker-ZXR/my-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/my-skills/skills/mri-hardware))
